Implementation details for FFT:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def convolve_self(p):
    n = len(p)
    M = 2*n -1
    N = next_pow2(M) # maybe use _obj_nfft1
    F = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)[:M]
    c[c < 0 & small] = 0? Due numerical negative tiny. Use np.maximum(c, 0). But negative can be -1e-16, max maybe off? Use `np.clip(c,0,None)`.
    return c
```
For conv(p, revp):
```
def convolve_ab(a,b):
    M = len(a)+len(b)-1
    N = next_pow2(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); B=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
    out=np.fft.irfft(A*B,N)[:M]
    # out can have length; due numerical maybe tiny negative. We need slices. For gradient, out real. Could clip? For grad, conv of nonnegative should nonnegative, but numerical negative tiny; clipping to 0 safe? It may slightly bias. Use np.maximum(out,0) for positivity.
    return np.maximum(out,0)
```

Actually linear minimizer chooses coordinate with most negative gradient. In our h= subgradient centered, p_new = p*exp(-η h) is multiplicative; FG would set w one-hot. Could do FISTA/FW. But multiplicative better.

Maybe use "separable concave approximation" of max? The max of quadratics not concave. But on active linearization, we can do projected gradient.

def mirror_descent_rel(p0, beta, l2w, eta0, maxit, deadline):
    p = normalize(p0)
    best=p; best_sc=score; n=OBJ_N? pass n.
    eta=eta0
    for it in range(maxit):
       time check
       c, L, grad_p = lse_grad_p(p)
       sc=2n max c
       update best
       if l2w: convJ=conv_ab(c,p[::-1],nfft2); grad_p += 4n*l2w*slice
       h=grad_p - n*? mean? h=grad_p - np.mean(grad_p)
       # maybe scale h for step: by norm
       hnorm = sqrt(mean(h^2))
       # clip h to avoid huge exp: h_clipped = np.clip(h, -0.1/h? )
       # We want eta*h reasonable. We can divide by max(1,hmax)
       hm = np.max(np.abs(h))
       if hm > 0: h = h / hm # then effective eta <= eta0/s? But line search will adjust.
       # Better normalize h to mean square 1: h /= sqrt(mean(h^2))
       scale_h = np.sqrt(np.mean(h*h)); if scale_h>0: h/=scale_h
       eta=eta0
       for lin in range(20):
           arg=-eta*h
           p_new = p * np.exp(arg); S=p_new.sum(); if S==0 break
           p_new/=S
           sc_new=score_from_p(p_new)

       p=p_new
       # optional: if no improvement for 100, perturb

Code for `combined_fun` details:
```
def combined_fun(x):
    n = OBJ_N
    if n == 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(0)
    beta = OBJ_BETA
    l2w = OBJ_L2W
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size != n-1 or not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n-1)
    y = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    y[:-1] = x
    y[-1] = 0.0
    # stable softmax
    yMax = np.max(y)
    if not np.isfinite(yMax):
       return 1e100, np.zeros(n-1,x?) # can't know size? x length.
    z = y - yMax
    # clip to avoid underflow and large exp (difference may be -inf)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    p = np.exp(z)
    s = p.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not finite:
       p = np.full(n, 1.0/n)
       s = 1
       # gradient zero? But x invalid; return large
    p /= s
    c = convolve_self_p_n(p, OBJ_NFFT1)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if beta <= 0.0:
       idx = int(np.argmax(c))
       pi = np.zeros(len(c)); pi[idx]=1
       L = cmax
    else:
       # For numerical, cmax maybe 0? If cmax <=1e-300: val=0?
       shift = beta * (c - cmax)
       if beta * (cmin?) large negative: np.exp underflows, okay
       expshift = np.exp(np.clip(shift, -745.0, 0.0))
       sexp = float(expshift.sum())
       if sexp <= 0:
           pi = np.zeros(len(c)); pi[int(np.argmax(c))]=1; L=cmax
       else:
           pi = expshift/sexp
           L = cmax + float(np.log(sexp))/beta
    val = 2.0 * n * L
    grad_p = np.zeros(n)
    if n > 0:
       par = convolve_ab(pi, p[::-1], OBJ_NFFT2)
       grad_p += 4.0 * n * par[n-1:n-1+n]
       if l2w != 0.0:
           cconv = convolve_ab(c, p[::-1], OBJ_NFTP2)
           grad_p += 4.0 * n * l2w * cconv[n-1:n-1+n]
           J = float(np.dot(c,c))
           val += l2w * (n * J - 0.5)
    gy = p * grad_p - p * float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
    gx = gy[:n-1] - gy[n-1]
    # clean nonfinite
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)):
       gx = np.zeros(n-1); val = 1e100
    return float(val), gx
```
Potential bug: If p all equal due clipping and x valid? Good.

If p_i=0, softmax gradient wrt logit_i = -p*dot = 0? If p_i=0 and dot finite, -0? But the term p_i*(...) =0. The derivative wrt a zero coordinate may be negative constant -p*dot? Wait formula gy_i = p_i grad_i - p_i sum; if p_i=0 =>0. Full gradient on unbounded y: if p_i=0, derivative 0? But because changing y_i can increase p_i, derivative should be something? For softmax limit p_i→0, ∂F/∂y_i = ∇F_j? Let's derive: p_i=e^{y_i}/S. ∂p_i/∂y_i=p_i, ∂p_j/∂y_i=-p_i p_j. As p_i→0, derivative = p_i(∂F/∂p_i - sum p_k∂F/∂p_k) ->0. So zero coordinate not changed by gradient (sticks). This is a problem: logits with underflow to zero become trapped because gradient zero for that coordinate. To avoid, maintain minimum p floor in objective. In stable softmax, we can add a small floor: `p = np.exp(z); floor = 1e-12/n; p = np.maximum(p, floor); p/=p.sum()`. But if p_i=1e-22, derivative tiny; maybe still stuck? To make zeros movable, add min_p=1e-12/n? That could be too large (1e-16 for n=7000) gradient ~. It won't affect score much. But if true optimum has some p extremely small (e.g., boundary p0 ~sqrt(M) ~ sqrt(0.75/n) ~0.01), not that small. So min_p 1e-12 safe. Let's think if there is an exact convex relaxation we could exploit for longer sequences: Since B_k have eigenvalues ±1, the quadratic q_k on nonnegative p can be transformed? If we define b_i = log a_i? Then q_k = ∑ exp(b_i+b_j). The max over k. Maybe optimizing in log domain with convex constraints? Constraint q_k≤t is sum of exponentials of pair sums: ∑ exp(θ_i+θ_j) ≤t. This is convex in θ? The exponential of sum of two variables is log-convex? log-sum of exponentials of linear functions is convex! Indeed log(∑ exp(θ_i+θ_j)) is convex (log-sum-exp of affine functions). Constraint log-sum-exp ≤ log t -? is convex! Wait q_k = ∑ over (i,j) a_i a_j where a_i = exp(θ_i). The set {θ: ∑ exp(θ_i+θ_j) ≤ t} is a log-sum-exp constraint, convex! Sum constraint? We were using l1 constraint on a; but we can impose log(∑ exp θ_i)=0 (sum a=1) which is also convex (log-sum-exp=0). Minimize t subject to these constraints is convex in θ! Ah! This is huge.

```
def normalized_score_G(p, beta, l2w, nfft1, nfft2):
    n=len(p)
    c=conv_self(p,nfft1)
    cmax = max
    ... pi
    L = cmax + log(sexp)/beta
    val = 2n*L
    H = 2*conv(pi, p_rev, nfft2)[n-1:n-1+n]
    D = dot(pi,c)
    G = 2n*(H - 2D)
    if l2w:
       J=dot(c,c); val += l2w*(n*J -0.5)
       Jp = 4*conv(c,p_rev,nfft2)[slice]
       G += l2w*n*(Jp -4*J)
    # ensure orthogonal: G -= np.dot(p,G)  # p sum1, subtract scalar? G = G - (p·G)*1? To be orthogonal to p, yes subtract scalar (p·G) from all entries (since ∑p=1). This also doesn't affect tangent? It changes constant; for logit gradient p∘G, constant removed by centering anyway. For raw gradient, removing constant? If G not orthogonal, need orthogonalize by subtract (p·G) * 1. This is valid because constant component doesn't affect sum-preserving p updates? For raw gradient of scale-invariant extension, constant component would violate; we can enforce. It may affect raw L-BFGS direction along scaling but good. So G = G - float(np.dot(p,G))
    return val, G
```

def a_raw_fun(x):
    n = A_OBJ_N
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size != n: return 1e100, zeros(n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): return 1e100, zeros
    x = np.maximum(x, 0)  # bounds but line search may
    S = x.sum()
    if S < 1e-12: return 1e100, zeros
    p = x / S
    # floor p for numerical? If we floor, normalizes again. p = np.maximum(p, 1e-15); p/=p.sum()
    # compute val, G via helper
    val, G = norm_score_G(p, A_OBJ_BETA, A_OBJ_L2W, A_OBJ_NFFT1, A_OBJ_NFFT2)
    grad = G / S
    # maybe clean nonfinite
    return float(val), grad
```

`norm_score_G` compute H and D. Code:
```
def norm_score_G(p, beta, l2w, nfft1, nfft2):
    n=len(p)
    p = np.maximum(p, PROB_FLOOR)
    p = p/p.sum()
    c = conv_self(p, nfft1)
    # c sum maybe not exactly 1; no need
    if beta <= 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(c)); pi=np.zeros(len(c)); pi[idx]=1.0; L=float(c[idx]); D=L
    else:
        cmax=float(np.max(c))
        shift=beta*(c-cmax)
        expshift=np.exp(np.clip(shift, -745, 0))
        sexp=expshift.sum()
        if sexp <=0: ...
        pi=expshift/sexp
        L=cmax + verify np.log(sexp)/beta
        D=float(np.dot(pi,c))
    val = 2.0*n*L
    src = p[::-1]
    par = conv_ab(pi, src, nfft2)
    H = 2.0*par[n-1:n-1+n]
    G = 2.0*n*(H - (2.0*D)*np.ones(n))
    if l2w !=0:
       J=float(np.dot(c,c))
       val += l2w*(n*J -0.5)
       jpar = conv_ab(c, src, nfft2)
       Jp = 4.0*jpar[n-1:n-1+n]
       G += l2w*n*(Jp - (4.0*J)*np.ones(n))
    # Orthogonalize G: G -= np.dot(p,G)  (since sum p=1)
    G = G - float(np.dot(p,G))
    return float(val), G
```
Is `D` for beta case: if beta<=0, cmax max, D=cmax, G=2n(H -2max), correct for exact max? H=2reverse at k, p·H=2max. Good.

   h = G
   # maybe add if beta==?

   rms = sqrt(mean(h*h)); if rms<1e-15 break
   h = h/rms
   h = np.clip(h,-4,4); h -= mean(h)
   eta=eta0
   accepted=False; p_new=None

      # if eta*h large, clip
      q = p * np.exp(np.clip(arg, -50, 50))

      S=q.sum(); if S<=0: break

      c_new = conv_self(q,nfft1)
      sc_new = 2*float(c_new.max())*q.size
      if sc_new < best_sc? For line search descent from current sc: require sc_new < sc - 1e-12 or (lin>3 and sc_new < sc)

      if sc_new < sc - 1e-12*max(1,sc): accept

      else eta*=0.5; if eta<1e-8 break
   if not accepted: break
   p=p_new
   # optional: occasional smooth? 
   if it % 50 ==0 and no_improve? maybe break after some.

def score_p(p, nfft1):
    if nfft1 is None: nfft1=next_pow2(2*len(p)-1)
    c=conv_self(p,nfft1)
    return 2*len(p)*float(c.max()), c

   configs = [(beta=50,l2w=0.5), (beta=200,l2w=0.3), (500,0.1), (2000,0.0), (10000,0.0), (30000,0.0)]

   After each, update best. Maybe alternate from perturbed best for low l2 configs:
   - If i==0: p_run = perturb(best,0.03)
   - i==1: p_run = best
   - i==2: perturb 0.02
   - i>=3: best
   This allows escape.
3. Mirror polish loop: For beta in [500,2000,10000], l2w [0.1,0.0,0.0], run mirror from perturbed/best for e.g. 200 iters each, update. Deadline `phase_deadline = start + 0.55*budget`.
   Use deadlines to limit each.
4. Iterated local search: while time_left > 60 and trial<30:
   - p_trial = perturb(best, scale=0.02+0.05*rng.random())
   - short mirror (beta=2000,l2w=0, eta=0.5,maxit=200, deadline=now+60)
   - short LBFGS (beta=20000,l2w=0, deadline=now+120)
   - update.
   This phase until 0.8.
5. Larger length:
   - If time_left>300: choose m_large = int(round(1.5*len(best))) maybe; resample. Set p_large. Run LBFGS beta=1000 l2w=0.1, then beta=10000, mirror. If improves maybe run another length 2n. Deadline add.
6. Final polish: from best, run high beta LBFGS and mirror with time_left>60.
7. Return best.

Need not over-optimize; code must not exceed 2400s. We can print progress.

Let's implement `local_lbfgs_run(p, beta, l2w, deadline, maxfun_cap)` top-level:
```
def lbfgs_refine_p(p, beta, l2w, deadline, maxfun_cap=20000):
    n=len(p); nfft1,nfft2=fft_sizes(n)
    set_a_obj(n,beta,l2w,nfft1,nfft2)
    _lbfgs_deadline=deadline
    x0 = np.clip(p,0.0,1.0)
    S=x0.sum(); if S<1e-12: x0=p/ (p.sum()+1)
    else: x0 = x0/S
    # If all zeros? no
    t0=time.monotonic()
    # estimate
    val0,grad0 = a_raw_fun(x0); t_est=max(1e-5, time.monotonic()-t0)
    time_left=deadline-time.monotonic()-15
    if time_left <=0: return score_from_p(p)
    maxfun=int(min(maxfun_cap, max(500, int(time_left*0.55/t_est))))
    maxiter=int(max(20, maxfun))
    bounds = [(0.0,1.0)]*n
    try:
        res = minimize(a_raw_fun, x0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, callback=lbfgs_callback, options={...})
    except Exception:
        res = None; x_cur=x0
    x_cur = res.x if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x') else x0
    # normalize out
    p_out = np.maximum(x_cur,0); S=p_out.sum()
    if S<1e-12: p_out=p
    else: p_out=p_out/S
    p_out=normalize_prob(p_out)
    sc = exact_score(p_out)
    return sc,p_out
```
Need `a_raw_fun` global callback; if minimize raises due system? catch.

        return True
    return False
```

Now, `mirror_descent_from_p` top-level. It uses `norm_score_G`, `conv_self`. It needs nffts. Use deadline. Use `maxit` and `eta0`.
```
def mirror_refine_p(p, beta, l2w, eta0, maxit, deadline):
    n=len(p); nfft1,nfft2=fft_sizes(n)
    p = normalize_prob(p)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_sc, c = exact_score_with_c(p,nfft1)
    eta=eta0; no_imp=0
    for it in range(maxit):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       c, val, G = norm_score_G(p,beta,l2w,nfft1,nfft2)
       sc = 2*n*float(c.max())
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best_sc=sc; best_p=p.copy(); no_imp=0
       else: no_imp+=1
       if no_imp > 200: break
       h = G - np.mean(G)
       rms = sqrt(mean(h*h))
       if rms < 1e-15: break
       h = h/rms
       # set eta maybe adapt: if it>0 and accepted quickly? no
       eta_try = eta
       accepted=False; p_new=None
       for ls in range(14):
           q = p * np.exp(np.clip(-eta_try*h, -50, 50))
           q = np.maximum(q, PROB_FLOOR); S=q.sum(); if S<=0: break
           q/=S
           sc_new = score_only(q,nfft1)
           if sc_new < sc - max(1e-12, 1e-10*sc):
               p_new=q; accepted=True; break
           if ls >= 5 and sc_new < sc:
               p_new=q; accepted=True; eta=eta_try*1.05?; break
           eta_try *=0.5
           if eta_try < 1e-8: break
           if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       if not accepted: break
       p=p_new
       # slowly reduce eta? eta = max(eta*0.999, 0.01)?
       eta = max(eta*0.999, 0.05)
    return best_sc,best_p
```
Need `score_only(p,nfft1)` returns 2n max(c) no returning c. `exact_score_with_c` similar.

The line search uses exact score and full FFT. If `sc` current exact is from c. Good.

Maybe mirror can get stuck if no descent. We call from perturbed.

def perturb_seq(p, rng, scale):
    n=len(p); strat=int(rng.integers(0,7))
    x = np.arange(n,dtype=float)/n
    if strat==0:
       p2 = p * np.exp(scale*2*rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif strat==1:
       f1=1+5*rng.random(); f2=5+20*rng.random(); ph1=2pi*rng.random(); ph2=2pi*rng.random()
       p2 = p*(1+scale*(0.5*np.cos(2πf1 x+ph1)+0.5*np.sin(2πf2 x+ph2)))
    elif strat==2:
       nb=int(3+rng.integers(0,8)); p2=p.copy(); for b: scale factor
    elif strat==3:
       # peak contributions reduce top
       c=conv_self(p); k=argmax; start/end; contrib = p[start:end+1][::-1]
       top_count=max(1,n//200)
       top=...; for top: p2[j]*= (1 - scale*0.5*rng.random())
       # add to bottom
       bottom=...
       distribute deficit? Simpler: p2 = p2 * (1+scale*rng.normal)?
    elif strat==4:
       p2 = p + scale*rng.standard_normal(n)
    elif strat==5:
       # block mirror of peak? 
       sel = rng.choice(n, size=max(1,n//8), replace=False); p2=p.copy(); p2[sel]*=1+scale*2*(rng.random(len(sel))-0.5)
    elif strat==6:
       # smooth Gaussian kernel convolution lowpass? Convolve p with kernel and renormalize? Not.
    p2=np.maximum(p2,0); S=p2.sum(); if S<=0: p2=p; else p2/=S
    return normalize_prob(p2)
```

def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

`fft_sizes(n)`.

`conv_self(p, nfft=None)`:
```
def conv_self(p, nfft=None):
  n=p.size
  if nfft is None: nfft=next_pow2(2*n-1)
  f=np.fft.rfft(p,nfft)
  c=np.fft.irfft(f*f,nfft)[:2*n-1]
  c=np.real(c)
  return np.maximum(c,0.0)
```
Potential p contains nan? assume.

`mirror_refine_p` use `norm_score_G` returning c. It normalizes p. Use `deadline`.
```
def mirror_refine_p(p,beta,l2w,eta0,maxit,deadline):
  n=len(p); nfft1,nfft2=fft_sizes(n)
  p=normalize_prob(p)
  best_sc,best_p = score_from_p(p,nfft1); best_p=p.copy()
  eta=eta0; no_improve=0
  for it in range(maxit):
    if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
    c,val,G=norm_score_G(...)
    sc=2*n*float(c.max())
    if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
       best_sc=sc; best_p=p.copy(); no_improve=0
    else:
       no_improve +=1
       if no_improve > 300: break
    h=G - np.mean(G)
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0,posinf=0.0,neginf=0.0)
    rms=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(h*h)))
    if rms < 1e-14: break
    h = h/rms
    h = np.clip(h, -4.0, 4.0)
    h -= float(np.mean(h))
    eta_try=eta
    accepted=False; p_new=None
    for ls in range(14):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2: break
       q = p * np.exp(np.clip(-eta_try*h, -50.0, 50.0))
       q=np.maximum(q,PROB_FLOOR); S=q.sum(); if S<=0: break
       q/=S
       sc_new = 2*n*float(np.max(conv_self(q,nfft1)))
       if sc_new < sc - max(1e-12, 1e-10*sc):
          accepted=True; p_new=q; break
       if ls >= 5 and sc_new < sc:
          accepted=True; p_new=q; break
       eta_try *= 0.5
       if eta_try < 1e-8: break
    if not accepted: break
    p=p_new
    eta = max(eta*0.998, 0.05)
  return best_sc,best_p
```
Maybe `G` can have large constant? We subtract mean. Good.

def update_best(score, p, label, best_score, best_a):
   if score < best_score and np.isfinite(score):
      print(f"  {label}: {score:.10f} n={len(p)} t={time...}??")
      best_a=np.clip(p,0,1000).copy(); best_score=float(score)
   return best_score,best_a
```

`build_initial(n,rng)`:
```
def build_initial(n,rng):
 x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)/max(1,n)
 if n==1: return np.array([1.0])
 inits=[]
 inits.append(np.ones(n)*0.5)
 inits.append(np.maximum(0.01, rng.random(n)))
 inits.append(1.0 - 2.0*np.abs(x-0.5))
 inits.append(0.2 + 0.8*np.cos(np.pi*x)**2)
 inits.append(np.exp(-4*(x-0.5)**2))
 inits.append(0.2 + 0.8*np.sin(np.pi*np.clip((x-0.2)/0.6,-1,1))**2)
# random beta shape
a=1+4*rng.random(); b=1+4*rng.random()
init=np.power(np.maximum(x,1e-6),a)*np.power(np.maximum(1-x,1e-6),b)+0.01
inits.append(init)
# low freq positive wave
freq=1+5*rng.random(); ph=2*pi*rng.random()
inits.append(np.maximum(0.1, 0.5+0.5*np.cos(2*pi*freq*x+ph)))
p=inits[int(rng.integers(len(inits)))]
return normalize_prob(p)
```

`resample_seq(p,m)`:
```
if m<=0: return np.ones(1)
if m==len(p): return p.copy()
x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,len(p),endpoint=True)
x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m,endpoint=True)
p_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,p)
return normalize_prob(p_new)
```
If p length1? handle.

`perturb_seq(p,rng,scale)`:
Simplify strategies 0-5; include one peak directed. Need ensure no `np.convolve` heavy; strategy 3 maybe uses conv_self; maybe too much if many. Keep 4 RNG strategies simple and one lowfreq.
```
def perturb_seq(p,rng,scale):
  n=len(p); strat=int(rng.integers(0,5))
  if strat==0: p2=p*np.exp(scale*2*rng.standard_normal(n))
  elif strat==1:
    f=1+8*rng.random(); ph=2*pi*rng.random(); amp=0.5+scale*rng.random()
    x=np.arange(n)/n
    p2=p*(1+scale*np.cos(2*pi*f*x+ph))
  elif strat==2:
    nb=int(3+rng.integers(0,8)); bs=max(1,n//nb); p2=p.copy()
    for b in range(min(nb, n//bs)):
      s=b*bs; e=min(s+bs,n); p2[s:e]*=1+2*scale*(rng.random()-0.5)
  elif strat==3:
    nsel=max(1,n//8); sel=rng.choice(n,nsel,False); p2=p.copy(); p2[sel]*=1+2*scale*(rng.random(nsel)-0.5)
  else:
    p2=p+scale*rng.standard_normal(n)
  return normalize_prob(p2)
```
Scale default 0.05. Multiplicative cos could negative if 1+scale cos; clip in normalize.

`beta_from_eff(eff,score,n)`:

if not finite score or score<=0: score=2.0
cmax=score/(2.0*n+n if?); cmax=max(1e-12,cmax/1?)
beta=eff/cmax
return float(np.clip(beta,1.0,2.0e6))
```
Where score is current exact best score. For p length n, cmax=score/(2n). Good.

Let's write `load_parent_sequence`:
```
def load_parent_sequence():
    try:
        if 'height_sequence_1' in globals():
            arr=np.asarray(globals()['height_sequence_1'], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if arr.size < 16: return None
            arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
            if arr.sum() > 1e-12:
                return normalize_prob(arr)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None
```

Let's draft phase code precisely.
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t_start + budget - 45.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent = load_parent_sequence()
    best_sc = np.inf
    best_a = None
    if parent is not None:
        if len(parent) > MAX_LEN:
            parent = resample_seq(parent, MAX_LEN)
        sc,p = score_from_p(parent)
        best_sc,best_a = update_best(sc,p,"parent",np.inf,None)
    else:
        n0 = 8192
        p = build_initial(n0,rng)
        sc,_ = score_from_p(p)
        best_sc,best_a = update_best(sc,p,"init",np.inf,None)
    if best_a is None:
        best_a = normalize_prob(np.ones(64)); best_sc = score_from_p(best_a)[0]
    n0 = len(best_a)
    usable = max(60.0, deadline - t_start)
    phase1_end = t_start + 0.22 * usable
    phase2_end = t_start + 0.78 * usable
    # Phase 1
    p_cur = best_a.copy()
    cur_sc = best_sc
    configs1 = [(1.0,0.5,120.0),(5.0,0.3,150.0),(20.0,0.1,180.0),(100.0,0.0,220.0),(300.0,0.0,250.0)]
    for idx,(eff,l2w,tlim) in enumerate(configs1):
        if time.monotonic() > phase1_end - 45: break
        current_n = len(best_a)
        if len(p_cur) != current_n:
            p_cur = resample_seq(best_a, current_n)
        if idx == 0 or eff >= 30.0:
            p_run = p_cur.copy()
        else:
            p_run = perturb_seq(p_cur, rng, 0.02 + 0.03 * rng.random())
        beta = beta_from_eff(eff, cur_sc if cur_sc < best_sc else best_sc, len(p_run))
        sub_deadline = min(phase1_end, time.monotonic() + tlim)
        if sub_deadline - time.monotonic() < 60: break
        sc,p = lbfgs_refine_p(p_run, beta, l2w, sub_deadline, 30000)
        best_sc,best_a = update_best(sc,p, f"orig_eff{int(eff)}_p{int(l2w*10)}", best_sc, best_a)
        if len(p) == len(p_cur) and sc < cur_sc:
               p_cur = p; cur_sc = sc
    if time.monotonic() + 80 < phase1_end:
        beta = beta_from_eff(100.0, cur_sc, len(p_cur))
        sc,p = mirror_refine_p(p_cur, beta, 0.05, 0.5, 800, min(phase1_end, time.monotonic()+200))
        best_sc,best_a = update_best(sc,p,"orig_mirror",best_sc,best_a)
        if len(p)==len(p_cur) and sc<cur_sc: p_cur=p; cur_sc=sc
```

Function definitions:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
       return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`fft_sizes`:
```
def fft_sizes(n):
    return next_pow2(2*n-1), next_pow2(3*n-2)
```

```
cmax = float(np.max(c))
if beta <= 0.0:
    pi = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = int(np.argmax(c))
    pi[idx]=1.0
    L=cmax; D=cmax
else:
    shift = beta * (c - cmax)
    expshift = np.exp(np.clip(shift, -745.0, 0.0))
    sexp = float(expshift.sum())
    if sexp <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sexp): ...
    pi = expshift / sexp
    L = cmax + float(np.log(sexp)) / beta
    D = float(np.dot(pi, c))
val = 2.0 * n * L
p_rev = p[::-1]
H_full = conv_ab(pi, p_rev, nfft2)
H = 2.0 * H_full[n-1:n-1+n]
G = 2.0 * n * (H - 2.0 * D)
if l2w != 0.0:
    J = float(np.dot(c, c))
    val += l2w * (n * J - 0.5)
    J_full = conv_ab(c, p_rev, nfft2)
    Jp = 4.0 * J_full[n-1:n-1+n]
    G += l2w * n * (Jp - 4.0 * J)
G = G - float(np.dot(p, G))
if not finite: fallback zeros.
return c, val, G

`lbfgs_refine_p`:
```
def lbfgs_refine_p(p, beta, l2w, deadline, maxfun_cap=30000):
    n=len(p)
    if n <=0: return inf, p
    nfft1,nfft2=fft_sizes(n)
    set_a_obj(n,beta,l2w)
    set_lbfgs_deadline(max(deadline, time.monotonic()))
    x0 = np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    x0 = np.where(np.isfinite(x0), x0, 0.0)
    x0 = np.clip(x0,0.0,1.0)
    S0=x0.sum()
    if S0 < 1e-12: x0 = normalize_prob(p).copy()
    else: x0 = x0 / S0
    # ensure not all floor? normalize? x0 sum1.
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try:
        val0, grad0 = a_raw_fun(x0)
        t_est = max(1e-5, time.monotonic()-t0)
    except Exception:
        t_est = 0.01
    time_left = deadline - time.monotonic() - 20.0
    if time_left <= 10.0:
        sc,_=score_from_p(x0)
        return sc, normalize_prob(x0)
    maxfun = int(min(maxfun_cap, max(300, int(time_left * 0.40 / t_est))))
    maxiter = int(max(20, maxfun))
    bounds = [(0.0,1.0)] * n
    options = {'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8,'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':50,'m':20}
    try:
       res = minimize(a_raw_fun, x0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, callback=lbfgs_callback, options=options)
       x_out = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
       x_out = x0
    p_out = np.where(np.isfinite(x_out), x_out, 0.0)
    p_out = np.maximum(p_out,0.0)
    Sout=float(p_out.sum())
    if Sout <1e-12: p_out=normalize_prob(p)
    else: p_out=p_out/Sout
    p_out=normalize_prob(p_out)
    sc,_=score_from_p(p_out)
    return sc, p_out
```
If t_est too low due val0 cached? no. `time_left*0.40/t_est` could be huge; cap. If t_est 0, maxfun 300. Good.

Potential issue: `minimize` may throw if callback returns True incorrectly? no.

```python
"""
Optimizer for non-negative step functions minimizing
    2 n max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

Strategy:
- Maintain a normalized probability vector p (sum = 1).
- Use a scale-invariant smooth objective:
    F(p) = 2n * LogSumExp_beta(c(p))  +  w * (n * ||c(p)||_2^2 - 0.5),
  where c(p) = p * p is the linear self-convolution.
- Optimize with L-BFGS-B in raw non-negative coordinates x, internally using p=x/sum(x).
- Use entropy/mirror line search in p-space for robust polish.
- Multi-start, perturbation, and a longer-length resampling phase to escape local basins.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

# -----------------------------
# globals for objective callback
# -----------------------------
PROB_FLOOR = 1e-15
SUM_EPS = 1e-6

A_OBJ_N = 0
A_OBJ_NFFT1 = 0
A_OBJ_NFFT2 = 0
A_OBJ_BETA = 1000.0
A_OBJ_L2W = 0.0

_LBFGS_DEADLINE = 0.0


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def fft_sizes(n):
    return next_pow2(2 * n - 1), next_pow2(3 * n - 2)


def set_a_obj(n, beta, l2w):
    global A_OBJ_N, A_OBJ_NFFT1, A_OBJ_NFFT2, A_OBJ_BETA, A_OBJ_L2W
    A_OBJ_N = int(n)
    if A_OBJ_N > 0:
        A_OBJ_NFFT1, A_OBJ_NFFT2 = fft_sizes(A_OBJ_N)
    else:
        A_OBJ_NFFT1 = 0
        A_OBJ_NFFT2 = 0
    A_OBJ_BETA = float(beta)
    A_OBJ_L2W = float(l2w)


def set_lbfgs_deadline(d):
    global _LBFGS_DEADLINE
    _LBFGS_DEADLINE = float(d)


def lbfgs_callback(*args):
    if time.monotonic() > _LBFGS_DEADLINE - 10.0:
        return True
    return False


def normalize_prob(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if p.size == 0:
        return p
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, PROB_FLOOR)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p[:] = 1.0 / float(p.size)
    else:
        p /= s
    return p


def conv_self(p, nfft=None):
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if nfft is None:
        nfft = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    f = np.fft.rfft(p, nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(f * f, nfft)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def conv_ab(a, b, nfft=None):
    la = int(a.size)
    lb = int(b.size)
    if la <= 0 or lb <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = la + lb - 1
    if nfft is None:
        nfft = next_pow2(m)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, nfft)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, nfft)[:m]
    out = np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def score_only(p, nfft1=None):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    if nfft1 is None:
        nfft1 = next_pow2(2 * p.size - 1)
    c = conv_self(p, nfft1)
    mc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mc):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * p.size * mc)


def score_from_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    nfft1 = next_pow2(2 * p.size - 1)
    c = conv_self(p, nfft1)
    mc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mc):
        return np.inf, c
    return float(2.0 * p.size * mc), c


def norm_score_G(p, beta, l2w, nfft1, nfft2):
    """
    Returns c, val, G.
    G is the scale-invariant gradient-like vector on the probability simplex:
      G is orthogonal to p and can be used by mirror methods / raw x-gradient.
    """
    p = normalize_prob(p)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), 1e100, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    c = conv_self(p, nfft1)
    if c.size == 0:
        return c, 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return c, 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta)
    l2w = float(l2w)

    if beta <= 0.0:
        pi = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        pi[idx] = 1.0
        L = cmax
        D = cmax
    else:
        shift = beta * (c - cmax)
        expshift = np.exp(np.clip(shift, -745.0, 0.0))
        sexp = float(expshift.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(sexp) or sexp <= 0.0:
            pi = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
            idx = int(np.argmax(c))
            pi[idx] = 1.0
            L = cmax
            D = cmax
        else:
            pi = expshift / sexp
            L = cmax + float(np.log(sexp)) / beta
            D = float(np.dot(pi, c))

    val = 2.0 * float(n) * float(L)

    p_rev = p[::-1]
    hp = conv_ab(pi, p_rev, nfft2)
    H = 2.0 * hp[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
    G = 2.0 * float(n) * (H - 2.0 * D)

    if l2w != 0.0:
        J = float(np.dot(c, c))
        val += l2w * (float(n) * J - 0.5)
        Jp = conv_ab(c, p_rev, nfft2)
        Jp = 4.0 * Jp[n - 1:n - 1 + n]
        G += l2w * float(n) * (Jp - 4.0 * J)

    # Enforce scale-invariance: remove component along p.
    G = G - float(np.dot(p, G))
    G = np.where(np.isfinite(G), G, 0.0)

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e100
    return c, float(val), G


def a_raw_fun(x):
    """
    Objective for L-BFGS in raw non-negative coordinates x.
    Internally uses p = x / sum(x), so the problem is scale invariant.
    """
    n = int(A_OBJ_N)
    if n <= 0:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)

    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size != n:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)

    S = float(x.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S < 1e-12:
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    p = x / S
    c, val, G = norm_score_G(p, A_OBJ_BETA, A_OBJ_L2W, A_OBJ_NFFT1, A_OBJ_NFFT2)

    # Weak penalty to keep scale sane; does not materially affect the scale-free objective.
    val += SUM_EPS * (S - 1.0) * (S - 1.0)
    grad = G / S
    grad = grad + 2.0 * SUM_EPS * (S - 1.0)

    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e100
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        val = 1e100
    return float(val), grad


def lbfgs_refine_p(p, beta, l2w, deadline, maxfun_cap=30000):
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0 or not np.isfinite(deadline):
        return score_only(p), np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)

    nfft1, nfft2 = fft_sizes(n)
    set_a_obj(n, beta, l2w)
    set_lbfgs_deadline(deadline)

    x0 = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    x0 = np.where(np.isfinite(x0), x0, 0.0)
    x0 = np.clip(x0, 0.0, 1.0)
    S0 = float(x0.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S0) or S0 <= 1e-12:
        x0 = normalize_prob(p)
    else:
        x0 = x0 / S0

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        a_raw_fun(x0)
        t_est = max(1e-5, time.monotonic() - t0)
    except Exception:
        t_est = 0.01

    time_left = float(deadline) - time.monotonic() - 20.0
    if time_left <= 15.0:
        p_out = normalize_prob(x0)
        return score_only(p_out), p_out

    maxfun = int(min(maxfun_cap, max(300, int(time_left * 0.40 / t_est))))
    maxiter = int(max(20, maxfun))

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    options = {
        'ftol': 1e-12,
        'gtol': 1e-8,
        'maxfun': maxfun,
        'maxiter': maxiter,
        'maxls': 50,
        'm': 20
    }

    x_out = x0
    try:
        res = minimize(
            a_raw_fun, x0,
            method='L-BFGS-B',
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            callback=lbfgs_callback,
            options=options
        )
        if res is not None and hasattr(res, 'x'):
            x_out = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        x_out = x0

    p_out = np.where(np.isfinite(x_out), x_out, 0.0)
    p_out = np.maximum(p_out, 0.0)
    Sout = float(p_out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(Sout) or Sout <= 1e-12:
        p_out = normalize_prob(p)
    else:
        p_out = p_out / Sout
    p_out = normalize_prob(p_out)

    sc = score_only(p_out)
    return sc, p_out


def mirror_refine_p(p, beta, l2w, eta0, maxit, deadline):
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return score_only(p), np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)

    nfft1, nfft2 = fft_sizes(n)
    p = normalize_prob(p)
    best_sc = score_only(p, nfft1)
    best_p = p.copy()

    eta = float(eta0)
    no_improve = 0
    deadline = float(deadline)

    for _ in range(int(maxit)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        c, val, G = norm_score_G(p, beta, l2w, nfft1, nfft2)
        sc = float(2.0 * n * max(np.max(c), 0.0))

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best_p = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 350:
                break

        h = G - float(np.mean(G))
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
        rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(h * h)))
        if not np.isfinite(rms) or rms < 1e-14:
            break

        h = h / rms
        h = np.clip(h, -4.0, 4.0)
        h = h - float(np.mean(h))

        eta_try = eta
        accepted = False
        p_new = p

        for ls in range(14):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2.0:
                break
            arg = np.clip(-eta_try * h, -50.0, 50.0)
            q = p * np.exp(arg)
            q = np.maximum(q, PROB_FLOOR)
            S = float(q.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-15:
                break
            q = q / S

            sc_new = score_only(q, nfft1)
            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < sc - max(1e-12, 1e-10 * max(sc, 1.0)):
                accepted = True
                p_new = q
                break
            if ls >= 5 and np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < sc:
                accepted = True
                p_new = q
                break

            eta_try *= 0.5
            if eta_try < 1e-8:
                break

        if not accepted:
            break

        p = p_new
        eta = max(eta * 0.998, 0.05)

    return float(best_sc), best_p


def update_best(score, p, label, best_score, best_a):
    if np.isfinite(score) and float(score) < float(best_score):
        p_out = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
        p_out = np.where(np.isfinite(p_out), p_out, 0.0)
        p_out = np.clip(p_out, 0.0, 1000.0)
        print(f"  NEW BEST [{label}] score={float(score):.10f} n={int(p_out.size)}")
        return float(score), p_out.copy()
    return best_score, best_a


def beta_from_eff(eff, score, n):
    if n <= 0:
        return 10000.0
    if not np.isfinite(score) or score <= 0.0:
        score = 2.0
    cmax = score / (2.0 * float(n))
    if cmax < 1e-12:
        cmax = 1e-12
    beta = float(eff) / cmax
    return float(np.clip(beta, 1.0, 5.0e6))


def load_parent_sequence():
    try:
        if 'height_sequence_1' in globals():
            arr = np.asarray(globals()['height_sequence_1'], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if arr.size >= 16:
                arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
                arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
                if arr.sum() > 1e-12:
                    return normalize_prob(arr)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def build_initial(n, rng):
    n = max(1, int(n))
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    inits = []

    inits.append(np.full(n, 0.5))
    inits.append(np.maximum(0.01, rng.random(n)))

    # V-shape / A-shape
    inits.append(1.0 - 2.0 * np.abs(x - 0.5))
    inits.append(2.0 * np.abs(x - 0.5) - 0.2)

    # Cosine / cosine-shaped
    inits.append(0.2 + 0.8 * np.cos(np.pi * x) ** 2)
    inits.append(np.maximum(0.1, np.cos(np.pi * x)))

    # Localized / gaussian
    inits.append(np.exp(-4.0 * (x - 0.5) ** 2))
    cx = 0.5 + 0.2 * (rng.random() - 0.5)
    inits.append(np.exp(-8.0 * (x - cx) ** 2))

    # Beta-like shape
    a = 1.0 + 5.0 * rng.random()
    b = 1.0 + 5.0 * rng.random()
    inits.append(np.power(np.maximum(x, 1e-6), a) * np.power(np.maximum(1.0 - x, 1e-6), b) + 0.01)

    # Smooth low-frequency wave
    f = 1.0 + 6.0 * rng.random()
    ph = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random()
    inits.append(np.maximum(0.05, 0.5 + 0.4 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + ph)))

    idx = int(rng.integers(0, len(inits)))
    return normalize_prob(inits[idx])


def resample_seq(p, m):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = max(1, int(m))
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 1:
        out = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        out[0] = float(p[0])
        return normalize_prob(out)
    if m == 1:
        out = np.zeros(1, dtype=np.float64)
        out[0] = float(p[0])
        return normalize_prob(out)

    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size, endpoint=True)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, endpoint=True)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    return normalize_prob(out)


def perturb_seq(p, rng, scale):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    scale = max(1e-4, float(scale))

    strat = int(rng.integers(0, 7))
    if strat == 0:
        p2 = p * np.exp(scale * 2.0 * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif strat == 1:
        x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        f1 = 1.0 + 8.0 * rng.random()
        f2 = 4.0 + 18.0 * rng.random()
        ph1 = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random()
        ph2 = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random()
        p2 = p * (1.0 + scale * (0.6 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * x + ph1) +
                                  0.4 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f2 * x + ph2)))
    elif strat == 2:
        p2 = p.copy()
        nb = 3 + int(rng.integers(0, 8))
        bs = max(1, n // nb)
        for b in range(min(nb, n // bs)):
            s = b * bs
            e = min(s + bs, n)
            p2[s:e] *= 1.0 + 2.0 * scale * (rng.random() - 0.5)
    elif strat == 3:
        nsel = max(1, n // 8)
        sel = rng.choice(n, size=nsel, replace=False)
        p2 = p.copy()
        p2[sel] *= 1.0 + 2.5 * scale * (rng.random(nsel) - 0.5)
    elif strat == 4:
        p2 = p + scale * rng.standard_normal(n)
    elif strat == 5:
        # multiplicative Gaussian low-pass-ish
        p2 = p * np.exp(0.35 * scale * rng.standard_normal(n))
    else:
        # random block flip scale
        p2 = p.copy()
        k = max(1, int(0.1 + 0.3 * rng.random()) * n)
        s = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - k)))
        p2[s:s + k] *= 1.0 - 0.25 * scale * (1.0 + rng.random())
        s2 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - k)))
        p2[s2:s2 + k] *= 1.0 + 0.35 * scale * (1.0 + rng.random())

    return normalize_prob(p2)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t_start + budget - 45.0
    usable = max(60.0, deadline - t_start)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    MAX_LEN = 20480

    best_sc = np.inf
    best_a = None

    parent = load_parent_sequence()
    if parent is not None:
        if parent.size > MAX_LEN:
            parent = resample_seq(parent, MAX_LEN)
        sc, _ = score_from_p(parent)
        best_sc, best_a = update_best(sc, parent, "parent", best_sc, best_a)
    else:
        n0 = 8192
        p0 = build_initial(n0, rng)
        sc, _ = score_from_p(p0)
        best_sc, best_a = update_best(sc, p0, "initial", best_sc, best_a)

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = normalize_prob(np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64))
        best_sc, _ = score_from_p(best_a)

    n0 = int(best_a.size)
    print(f"Starting n={n0}, best={best_sc:.10f}, budget={budget:.1f}s")

    # ------------------
    # Phase 1: polish base length
    # ------------------
    phase1_end = t_start + 0.22 * usable
    p_cur = best_a.copy()
    cur_sc = best_sc

    configs1 = [
        (1.0, 0.5, 120.0),
        (5.0, 0.3, 150.0),
        (20.0, 0.1, 180.0),
        (100.0, 0.0, 220.0),
        (300.0, 0.0, 250.0),
    ]

    for idx, (eff, l2w, tlim) in enumerate(configs1):
        if time.monotonic() > phase1_end - 45:
            break
        sub_deadline = min(phase1_end, time.monotonic() + tlim)
        if sub_deadline - time.monotonic() < 70:
            break

        use_n = int(best_a.size)
        if int(p_cur.size) != use_n:
            p_cur = resample_seq(best_a, use_n)

        if idx == 0 or eff >= 30.0:
            p_run = p_cur.copy()
        else:
            p_run = perturb_seq(p_cur, rng, 0.02 + 0.03 * rng.random())

        est_score = cur_sc if np.isfinite(cur_sc) else best_sc
        beta = beta_from_eff(eff, est_score, len(p_run))
        sc, p = lbfgs_refine_p(p_run, beta, l2w, sub_deadline, 30000)
        best_sc, best_a = update_best(sc, p, f"base_eff{int(eff)}_{int(l2w*10)}", best_sc, best_a)

        if int(p.size) == use_n and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_sc:
            p_cur = p
            cur_sc = sc

    if time.monotonic() + 90 < phase1_end:
        beta = beta_from_eff(100.0, cur_sc, len(p_cur))
        sc, p = mirror_refine_p(p_cur, beta, 0.05, 0.5, 900,
                                min(phase1_end, time.monotonic() + 220))
        best_sc, best_a = update_best(sc, p, "base_mirror", best_sc, best_a)
        if int(p.size) == len(p_cur) and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur_sc:
            p_cur = p
            cur_sc = sc

    # ------------------
    # Phase 2: longer lengths
    # ------------------
    phase2_end = t_start + 0.78 * usable

    larges = []
    for target in (1.5, 1.8, 2.2, 2.8):
        m = int(round(n0 * target / 256.0) * 256)
        if n0 < m <= MAX_LEN:
            larges.append(m)
    if n0 < 16384 and 16384 <= MAX_LEN:
        larges.append(16384)

    # de-duplicate
    uniq = []
    for m in larges:
        if m not in uniq:
            uniq.append(m)

    # Prefer a 15k-18k length if present, then another candidate.
    selected = []
    for m in uniq:
        if 13000 <= m <= 19000:
            selected.append(m)
            break
    if not selected and uniq:
        selected.append(uniq[-1])
    elif len(uniq) >= 2:
        for m in uniq:
            if m != selected[0]:
                selected.append(m)
                break
    larges = selected[:2]

    for m in larges:
        if time.monotonic() >= phase2_end - 180:
            break

        p_local = resample_seq(best_a, m)
        local_sc, _ = score_from_p(p_local)
        best_m_sc = local_sc
        p_best_m = p_local
        best_sc, best_a = update_best(local_sc, p_local, f"resample_{m}", best_sc, best_a)

        configs2 = [
            (1.0, 0.5, 180.0),
            (5.0, 0.3, 210.0),
            (20.0, 0.1, 250.0),
            (100.0, 0.0, 300.0),
            (300.0, 0.0, 330.0),
        ]

        for eff, l2w, tlim in configs2:
            if time.monotonic() >= phase2_end - 120:
                break
            sub_deadline = min(phase2_end, time.monotonic() + tlim)
            if sub_deadline - time.monotonic() < 90:
                break

            if eff >= 10.0:
                p_run = p_best_m.copy()
            else:
                p_run = perturb_seq(p_best_m, rng, 0.02 + 0.04 * rng.random())

            beta = beta_from_eff(eff, best_m_sc, m)
            sc, p = lbfgs_refine_p(p_run, beta, l2w, sub_deadline, 60000)
            best_sc, best_a = update_best(sc, p, f"long_eff{int(eff)}_{int(l2w*10)}_n{m}",
                                          best_sc, best_a)
            if int(p.size) == m and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_m_sc:
                best_m_sc = sc
                p_best_m = p

        if time.monotonic() + 100 < phase2_end:
            beta = beta_from_eff(100.0, best_m_sc, m)
            sc, p = mirror_refine_p(p_best_m, beta, 0.0, 0.5, 1200,
                                    min(phase2_end, time.monotonic() + 260))
            best_sc, best_a = update_best(sc, p, f"long_mirror_n{m}", best_sc, best_a)
            if int(p.size) == m and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_m_sc:
                best_m_sc = sc
                p_best_m = p

        # A couple of short iterated local searches at this length.
        for _trial in range(2):
            if time.monotonic() >= phase2_end - 130:
                break
            p_trial = perturb_seq(p_best_m, rng, 0.02 + 0.04 * rng.random())

            beta = beta_from_eff(30.0, best_m_sc, m)
            sc, p = mirror_refine_p(p_trial, beta, 0.05, 0.5, 500,
                                    min(phase2_end, time.monotonic() + 130))
            best_sc, best_a = update_best(sc, p, f"long_ILS_mirror_n{m}", best_sc, best_a)
            if int(p.size) == m and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_m_sc:
                best_m_sc = sc
                p_best_m = p

            if time.monotonic() + 80 < phase2_end:
                beta = beta_from_eff(100.0, best_m_sc, m)
                sc, p = lbfgs_refine_p(p_best_m, beta, 0.0,
                                       min(phase2_end, time.monotonic() + 180), 40000)
                best_sc, best_a = update_best(sc, p, f"long_ILS_lbfgs_n{m}", best_sc, best_a)
                if int(p.size) == m and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_m_sc:
                    best_m_sc = sc
                    p_best_m = p

    # ------------------
    # Phase 3: final polish
    # ------------------
    p_final = best_a.copy()

    final_configs = [
        (20.0, 0.05, 130.0),
        (100.0, 0.0, 170.0),
        (300.0, 0.0, 220.0),
    ]

    for eff, l2w, tlim in final_configs:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 70:
            break
        sub_deadline = min(deadline - 10.0, time.monotonic() + tlim)
        if sub_deadline - time.monotonic() < 70:
            break

        use_n = len(p_final)
        if int(best_a.size) != use_n:
            p_final = resample_seq(best_a, use_n)

        beta = beta_from_eff(eff, best_sc, use_n)
        sc, p = lbfgs_refine_p(p_final, beta, l2w, sub_deadline, 60000)
        best_sc, best_a = update_best(sc, p, f"final_eff{int(eff)}_{int(l2w*10)}",
                                      best_sc, best_a)
        p_final = p

    if time.monotonic() + 80 < deadline:
        beta = beta_from_eff(300.0, best_sc, len(best_a))
        sc, p = mirror_refine_p(best_a, beta, 0.0, 0.35, 900, deadline - 20.0)
        best_sc, best_a = update_best(sc, p, "final_mirror", best_sc, best_a)

    if time.monotonic() + 70 < deadline:
        beta = beta_from_eff(100.0, best_sc, len(best_a))
        sc, p = lbfgs_refine_p(best_a, beta, 0.0, deadline - 15.0, 30000)
        best_sc, best_a = update_best(sc, p, "final_lbfgs", best_sc, best_a)

    # ------------------
    # Final safe output
    # ------------------
    out = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S < 1e-12:
        out = np.full(64, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
        S = float(out.sum())
    out = out / S

    final_list = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in out]

    verified = np.inf
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
    except Exception:
        verified = np.inf

    print("=" * 60)
    print(f"FINAL internal={best_sc:.10f}, verified={verified:.10f}, n={len(final_list)}, "
          f"time={time.monotonic() - t_start:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 60)

    return final_list
```
